# F.W4 — CARRY TABLE (intake for the X·F wave spec)

**Wave**: X.F.W4 — the saturated per-component frontend audit on the uplifted tree (a11y · motion/PRM · state-truth · semantics · contrast · geometry · dead code · hygiene) + the `BasisCanvas`↔`FourierField` convergence study + reduced-motion clock gating + the dead-dep/vestigial-shadcn scrub + the **vitest-floor DECISION**.
**Authority**: `docs/tranches/X/COHESION.md` §0/§1 SS-3, §0a boundary 2026-08-25 · `formation/fourier/CENSUS-2026-08-03.md:193-195` (the wave sketch) · `audit/codex-provenance/INTAKE-ADJUDICATION-2026-08-03.md` (F.W4 carried rows) · the 66 adjudicated `registry/adjudicated/fr-*.md` records at `35fc8ebf`.
**Law**: M-25 (agglomerate — dedupe by identity, carry cure-shape locks and sequencing edicts, route by mechanism; transcription-only is DEFECTIVE). L-20 (validate against `docs/precepts/` latest at authoring). Born-RED: every gate opens RED with its witness named. L-19: no gate that does not name a real witness.
**Execution gate**: status stays `planned`. **NOTHING opens product source.** `/Users/mkbabb/Programming/fourier-analysis` is READ-ONLY. This file is intake, not a spec; it stamps nothing.

## §0 Derivation-law preamble (the spec MUST open with this)

The audit model this wave inherits is the **cured** deriver model, not Codex's R3 one. Four blind spots and two denominators are law before any percentage is published:

- **BS-1 · callsite-keyed blindness** (intake **R5-7/R6-5**, standing F.W4 carry). Loop evidence keyed to component callsites drops native element loops entirely. Witnesses: `PaperSidebar` ToC (3 nested `<li v-for>`, live 65/87/105) · fr-AdminAuditLog AA-30 (1 native `<div v-for>`, ~104 nodes) · fr-AdminFlaggedPanel FR-AFP-50 (both loops native-`<div>`-hosted, 33-site denominator) · fr-ConvergenceLegend D-L5 (entire variable surface = one native `v-for`, ≤102 rows, instance-keyed reads 0) · fr-ConvergencePlot L§5.3 · fr-GalleryFeaturedCarousel FR-GFC-28 · fr-PaperArticleWindow PAW-36. **Refinement (fr-AdminUserList FR-AUL-54, rides the carry, never re-booked): count native loops by DIRECTIVE and re-parent component callsites underneath them — an element-name-keyed `NATIVE_TEMPLATE_LOOP` cure re-inherits the blind spot (no `<li>` in AdminUserList).**
- **BS-2 · dynamic-`:is` blindness** (intake **R3-10**, F.W4 carry). All **6** live dynamic-`:is` families budgeted; per-component denominators must resolve `:is` candidate sets or inherit an R5-7-shaped blind spot on a second axis (fr-AppHeader FR-AH-47 rider; fr-CoefficientsSpectrum i-1 live at `:132`; fr-AnimationControls L-8 — two `v-if/v-else` icon twins invisible to an `:is`-keyed budget).
- **BS-3 · producer-internal loops** (fr-ConvergenceTimeline L·§3): a producer that fans out over a consumer array (`renderList` inside a compiled chunk) is invisible behind the component boundary — harmless at n=1, systematically undercounting elsewhere. **fr-SliderControl L-§R scope-law carry**: a `web/src`-scoped census reports ZERO interactive slider nodes for a surface with 14+; derive component-boundary loops from first-party `@mkbabb/*` d.ts + compiled render functions (closed, versioned denominator). fr-VisualizationView §4 same.
- **BS-4 · `url(#id)` reference edges** (fr-SvgFilters L-5, method amendment): filter/mask/clipPath/gradient/pattern/marker edges are representable by NO import- or callsite-graph. F.W4 must derive them (the 178-line `SvgFilters` survivor is the proof).
- **LOOP-SOURCE PROVENANCE** (fr-ContourSettings i-8, NO-WAVE-OWNER → F.W4 execution carry): the dual of BS-1 — a loop whose SOURCE is an untyped literal disagreeing with the backend enum is invisible to callsite-keyed AND native-loop-keyed derivations alike. The audit needs a **loop-source-provenance counter (literal vs typed domain)**. Cross-ref fr-BasisSelector i-2. **fr-ConvergencePlot L§5.3 bound-carry**: native-loop counting must record the loop's BOUND (1..100 UI / `le=200` wire), not merely its existence. **fr-HarmonicLevelGrid L:S-3 cardinality caveat**: one SITE ⇒ 12–14 INSTANCES, data-dependent mid-drag. **fr-EasingPicker L/§6**: object-source arity across two module hops — an 18-instance surface derives as 2. **fr-GalleryCardModal GCM-54 / L-17**: canonical-form collapse over duplicated derivations is a second loop-denominator leak.
- **DISCLOSURE-STATE GATE** (fr-CollapsibleSection m-14, inverted polarity): source-derived loop evidence OVER-counts what a DOM census sees behind a closed section (`FunctionInput:157` Tooltip `v-for` inside the Function section; `EqCoefficientsPanel :default-open="false"` ⇒ CoefficientsSpectrum tooltips absent at boot). **Record enclosing disclosure state, or force-mount, before comparing derived to live.**
- **CORRECTED DENOMINATORS.** Routes = **9 records** (7 lazy component + 2 redirects + 1 alias) — intake **X-2**, census §3a's "8 routes, all lazy" is WRONG. API = **45 = 30 public-non-admin + 13 admin + 1 app + 1 gallery** (intake X-3). Member scope = intake **X-9**: publish ONE member-scope law BEFORE any percentage (R3's 189 vs R4–R6's 318 are both self-consistent; neither is "the" denominator). **Unreachable code is not a denominator**: fr-GalleryFeaturedCarousel FR-GFC-9 puts `GalleryMarquee:29,36,52` in code whose mount predicate is false for all inputs — the 17-native/16-component split and the R6 reconciliation need re-derivation before any spec quotes them. **Zero-cells are EXPORTED from F.W5 (P-9), copied into F.W2's and F.W4's denominators, one home two citations**: App API-inert · MobileFloatingToc · CollapsibleSection · PaperSearch/Input/Dropdown · MorphShapePreview (structurally unreachable) · CanvasControlsDock/AnimationControls zero migration cost · **UserSlugBar FR-USB-38 — do NOT book into the F.W2 migration surface.**
- **REPAIR-UNIT SIZING** (fr-EquationResult DISSENT-1, the ColorSpaceSelector addendum §2 law): **a cure inherits no severity from the defects it would carry.** The dedup MINOR stays MINOR — but the vehicle status books as a ROUTING fact. Corollary in force here: `renderLatex()` is the DESIGNATED CURE VEHICLE for FR-EQR-4/-15/-16/-19; if F.W4 patches four sites instead of routing through one home, reader-2's MAJOR was the truer reading and FR-EQR-17 escalates at this wave's open.
- **EVIDENCE STANDARD** (intake §6 item 5(i)): a registry audit that re-hashes bytes but never re-derives products proves the bytes unchanged, not the summary true. Identity ordering is `UTF8_BYTEWISE_CODEPOINT`, never `localeCompare`. **D-19: every line anchor in this table is re-resolved before any cure** — the fr-EquationResult K-10 precedent (challenge-C's whole-document anchors displaced ~2 lines; re-pin from D/L/the record, never from C) and fr-ContourSettings K-9 (challenge-C cites `:492-506` past EOF on a 470-line file).

Format: `**id** ⟨record · merged source ids⟩ SEV — mechanism. CURE+LOCKS. SEQ. DISSENT.`
Severity as adjudicated. Every id keeps its identity for life; a row banked in N records appears ONCE citing all witnesses. `⇢` = same-commit rider. `⊘` = cure-shape lock (a cure that violates it ships a regression).

### C · fr-AdminUserList (30 rows to this wave)

- **FR-AUL-1** ⟨AUL D-04/L-1/C-B1⟩ **BLOCKER** — selection survives a search or sort refilter on page 1 (the default view): `watch(page, clearSelection)` is the sole invalidation edge and `loadPage(1)` from page 1 is an `Object.is`-guarded identity write. The batch cascade then fires at slugs the operator cannot see, behind a confirm that names **no** target while the singular path names its exact victim in `font-mono` — the more dangerous action carries the weaker confirmation. Backend is a **hard** cascade with no restore. The repo convicts itself: `GalleryView.vue:204-205` clears the structurally identical `selectedHashes` on BOTH invalidating events. **CURE**: clear on `[searchQuery, sortMode]` (or in the fetchFn) **AND** enumerate/preview the batch targets in the confirm; **preserve the snapshot-at-ask discipline** (it froze a set that was already wrong). **⊘ FR-AUL-22 is the sibling edge this cure does NOT close — booked separately so the fix-by-watch does not close the ledger over it.**
- **FR-AUL-2** ⟨AUL D-08/L-2/C-B3 + LC-M1⟩ MAJOR — the composable's `error` is produced and never consumed. **Sharpened**: `page` is written before the fetch and `items` only on success ⇒ a failed reload renders the PREVIOUS page's rows under the NEW page number with no signal; "No users found" is only the first-load face. AdminAuditLog omits `error` identically. CURE: consume `error` with a distinct failure state; re-clamp/restore `page` on failure (or write it only on success).
- **FR-AUL-4** ⟨AUL D-14/L-4/C-M5⟩ MAJOR — four of five post-mutation reloads call bare `loadPage()`, skipping the clamp; server does not clamp either. Render collision: "No users found" + pager "3 / 2" + "40 total" simultaneously. **⊘ preserve `performPrune`'s deliberate `loadPage(1)` reset** (superlative S-7 — the author knew the rule).
- **FR-AUL-5** ⟨AUL D-02/L-15/C-m1/C-m2⟩ MAJOR — the documented `data-some` indeterminate mechanism does not exist (grep = 2: comment + binding), `data-selected` styles nothing on these rows, and the primitive ships indeterminate WHOLE. At 5-of-20 the gate to a hard cascade renders fully unchecked and clicking it **adds the other 15**. CURE: `someOnPageSelected && !allOnPageSelected ? "indeterminate" : allOnPageSelected`; delete the dead attribute and the false comment; give selected rows a real style.
- **FR-AUL-7** ⟨AUL D-05/C-m8⟩ MAJOR — hard-coded Tailwind literals fail 1.4.3 in light mode on the SOLE "suspended" signifier (`bg-red-500/20` + `text-red-400` over light `--card` ≈2.0:1 at the 10 px rung) and on the prune control (≈1.3:1) — reaching past the repo's own ratified `--viz-amber` axe-carry to literals ~2.5× worse. Direction and ~3× margins certain; exact composited ratios → SS-13 (Tailwind v4 ships oklch). Post-uplift the `./metric`/`./chip` homes exist. **fr-EquationView D·D-M7's palette-literal limb folds here.**
- **FR-AUL-8** ⟨AUL D-09/L-19/C-m5/C-m4⟩ MAJOR — the "loading overlay" is a full content swap on every 300 ms typing pause and every mutation, inside a scrolling ancestor, while the select-all header and batch toolbar keep rendering stale chrome over the blank; `loading` is a boolean not a counter, so concurrent loads (routine under FR-AUL-3) re-mount the list over stale items mid-request. `Skeleton` ships at the pin and survives 7.0.0. CURE: dim-in-place/`aria-busy`/skeleton + token or counter.
- **FR-AUL-9** ⟨AUL D-10 + DU/LC-M6⟩ MAJOR — search results never announced, and BOTH live regions are conditionally mounted with their content (the spinner's `role="status"` inside `v-if="loading"`; the pager's `aria-live` inside `<nav v-if="pageCount > 1">`, which unmounts exactly when the result set gets small). `{{ total }}` is behind the same gate; "No users found" is in no live region. **CURE: ONE permanently-mounted `sr-only` live region outside both gates** — discharges both halves. Cohort = FR-AFP-43, GAB-10 (live-region half), FR-MSP-9.
- **FR-AUL-15** ⟨AUL L-5/C-m6⟩ MAJOR — no unmount teardown under a mount pattern that guarantees unmount: the raw `searchTimer` outlives the component and fires `loadPage(1)` against a dead scope; `deactivateAdmin()` both unmounts and clears the token, so the orphan timer runs `getAdminToken()!` on null. CURE: `onScopeDispose` clears the timer; abort in-flight on dispose. **Family**: FR-GSB-20 (rider), fr-GalleryView L-5 (DEMOTED to MINOR — no reachable user-visible consequence; both readers' MAJOR preserved in DISSENT), PSM-22.
- **FR-AUL-19** ⟨AUL D-11⟩ MAJOR — control-height ladder bypassed by literals: 34/32/36 px stagger in one header row; six icon buttons at `h-6`/`h-7` overriding `size="icon"` (with `icon-sm` byte-equivalent and unused); on coarse pointers the floor machinery turns each 24 px button into a 44 px box painted with a 14 px glyph, taken out of the truncating slug column. CURE: `size` props, not literals. Coarse-pointer consequence → SS-13. **Cohort: AA-22, FR-AFP-28, FR-USB-3, D/M-12.**
- **FR-AUL-21 rider** ⟨AUL DU-miss + L-13-falsifier⟩ — **F.W4 rider on the F.W5 closed-domain row**: the template must render **unknown statuses VISIBLY, never as healthy**. Same mechanism as GAB-16.
- **FR-AUL-22** ⟨AUL LC-M4⟩ MAJOR — a single-row delete or suspend never clears that row's slug from `selected` ⇒ the toolbar reports a selection with zero checked boxes and a later batch ships the dead slug (`deleted_count` contributes 0 → "Deleted 0 user(s)" as a green success). **Survives FR-AUL-1's ranked cure.** CURE: mutations remove their victims from `selected`, or selection re-derives against the loaded page. - **FR-AUL-24** ⟨AUL DU-miss⟩ MAJOR — every destructive action dumps keyboard focus to `<body>`: reka captures the pre-open active element and refocuses it on close, but `confirmPending` closes the dialog, awaits the mutation, and the success path's `loadPage()` unmounts the entire `v-if="!loading"` list including the row button focus was restored to. ### G · the `/equation` convergence instrument — fr-ConvergencePlot · fr-ConvergenceLegend · fr-ConvergenceTimeline · fr-CoefficientsPanel · fr-CoefficientsSpectrum · fr-EqCoefficientsPanel · fr-FrequencyGraph · fr-HarmonicLevelGrid

In-page the double loop self-heals via one pause; **unmount-with-playing-true is the irrecoverable arm.** In-tree counter-example carries all three missing safety properties (`stores/animation.ts:52,:59-62`). CURE: port the store's two guards + `playing.value = false` in `onUnmounted` (discharges C-15 too). **Attribution per the fr-CT ruling: this is ConvergencePlot's OWN row.** Same edit as L-m5 (`animDuration` mid-flight rebasing).

Survived because there is no seam at which the identity-lerp could be observed. - **FR-CP-LM2 ⊕ M-L1** ⟨ConvergencePlot L-M2; missed R-L⟩ MAJOR — `props.domain` feeds `omega`, `xGrid`, the closing sample and `maxX` and has **NO watcher**, while the callsite binds live refs against last-successful-response data ⇒ **the divergence window is UNBOUNDED** (`onDomainInput` writes the model and emits nothing): a typed domain edit renders wrong-frequency curves, a backwards closing segment, or a mirrored projection **until the user presses Compute, which may be never**. And there is no domain validation anywhere: `parseDomainValue` accepts any finite pair with no `start < end` check ⇒ reversed domain mirrors every mark AND `niceStep(negative)` returns NaN so **every vertical grid line silently vanishes**; degenerate domain ⇒ `omega = Infinity` ⇒ NaN blank canvas. **Contrast that makes it a code-quality row: every divisor in `lib/harmonics.ts` IS guarded (S-6) while the component's two are not.** CURE: watch `domain` (+`expression`); source `domain` from `result` at the callsite; validate at FunctionInput + guard the two divisors.

### H · the `/equation` authoring surface — fr-EquationView · fr-EquationResult · fr-EquationPanel · fr-EquationModeToggle · fr-FunctionInput · fr-NotationPills · fr-ExportModal

- **EV-B-1 (= FR-EMT-1)** ⟨EquationView L·B-1/C·D-05; EquationModeToggle L-B2/C-1/D-9⟩ **BLOCKER** — **the Notation control is INERT in the DEFAULT display mode.** `eqMode` defaults `"sigma"`; `activeLatex` prefers `displayLatexSigma`, whose only writers are the cache init and `doCompute`; the notation path routes ONLY to `doSimplify`, which writes `displayLatex` alone; `SimplifyResponse = {latex, energy_captured, term_count}` on BOTH sides — **no sigma arm** — while the server proves sigma IS notation-dependent (`equations.py:96 render_latex_sigma(terms, req.notation)`) and budget-INdependent. **Self-sealing**: `lastDisplayKey` advances on success so every retry is suppressed, and the energy badge DOES move, so the control looks alive. `activeLatex` guards emptiness, not freshness ⇒ a stale-but-truthy sigma wins. **⊘ CURE-CONSTRAINT (M-CK, seat-ratified): `computeKey()` UNDER-DETERMINES the request — 4 fields returned vs 7 posted, including `notation` and `budget`, both response-determining. BOTH prescribed B-1 cures (route notation through doCompute; revive the memo) WALK INTO IT: `doCompute(false)` would see an unchanged key on a notation change and early-return into doSimplify, REPRODUCING B-1 one layer down. Normalize at ONE seam, then key** — and R2-N5 binds in: `computeKey()` reads the UNTRIMMED expression while the request posts the trimmed one, so states differing only in whitespace produce different keys and byte-identical requests.
- **EV-B-2 (= FunctionInput L-B1/C-1)** ⟨EquationView L·B-2/C·D-01⟩ **BLOCKER** — **`budget` walks past the contract bound `le=50` in ONE Harmonics drag from shipped defaults**: the drag flips `autoHarmonics` off so `vizHarmonics === nHarmonics` (max 100), and the rescale watcher's second branch yields `budget(v) = v − 10` on a monotone up-drag (20→10, 30→20, 61→**51**, 100→90; a single track-click jump from defaults yields exactly 50). Both pydantic bounds are `ge=2, le=50`. Through doSimplify the 422 lands in `/* silent */`; through doCompute it renders as the empty-or-useless banner; and **`budget` restores from sessionStorage — the escalation PERSISTS into the next session.** **⊘ CURE-CONSTRAINT (M-BR): the slider-side clamp is KILLED as the cure — the `:151` watcher has no `immediate:true`, `autoHarmonics` is not persisted and resets true, and the Display-terms slider clamps on READ (`:model-value="Math.min(...)"`) but NOT on WRITE (`budget = $event` raw) ⇒ the control DISPLAYS a number the component will not SEND, and the restore path never passes through the slider. Clamp at the ref or the two request sites.** **F.W5–W8**: one shared bound constant. **EV-L·M-6** is the ENGINE: the rescale guard is a TAUTOLOGY (`budget <= oldV` holds in EVERY reachable steady state because the slider's own `:max` IS `vizHarmonics`), so exactly one branch fires on every change and the user's explicit Display-terms choice is rewritten on every step of a drag → make the guard say what the comment says, or delete the rescale.
- **EV-L·M-4/C·C-34 (RD-1) ⊕ R2-N6 ⊕ R2-r4** ⟨EquationView⟩ MAJOR — `retryOn429` is declared on `CoreFetchOptions` and ABSENT from `ApiFetchOptions`, and `apiFetch` is the only exported wrapper ⇒ **the equation calls cannot opt out even in principle**, with `retryOn429` defaulting TRUE and `MAX_RATE_LIMIT_RETRIES = 2`. The compute-saturation 429 is raised only AFTER a 30 s semaphore wait — **a backpressure signal** — and arrives STAMPED `RateLimit-Reset ≈ 60`, so each retry sleeps 30 s: **worst case ≈ 2×30 s client + 3×30 s server ≈ 150 s with `computing` pinned true, no `AbortSignal.timeout`, and the spinner reading "Computing…" throughout, auto-amplifying against the endpoint that asked for relief.** SCOPED: in the SATURATION arm each admitted retry records a limiter hit, so **one user gesture can burn up to 3 of the 5/min shared compute budget**; the LIMITER-429 arm burns nothing. **R2-N6 widens L·M-2's window**: the backoff sleep is a bare `setTimeout` promise with no `signal.aborted` check, no abort listener, no `clearTimeout` ⇒ a SUPERSEDED request does not settle for ≤30 s, then its `finally` clears `computing` under a successor — **the clobber window widens from a microtask to thirty seconds and can cross TWO request generations**; with no teardown, a route-leave during backoff leaves a live timer holding the whole closure. **Seat adjacency catch: fr-EquationPanel's RATIFIED superlative praises the signal hoisted OUTSIDE the retry loop — carry that contradiction into the cure.**
- **EV-D·D-B4 (= L·M-5 = C·D-07)** ⟨EquationView⟩ MAJOR — **the unified busy state was designed and never wired**: `loading` has ZERO consumers (the template reads `computing`/`error`/`result` only) and doSimplify's failure path is a terminal no-op (`/* silent */`) — **opposite error postures at two async seams 40 lines apart, and the SILENT one is the seam the user drives most** (every notation click, every budget drag). B-2 supplies a one-drag 422 through exactly this seam; no toast surface exists in the subtree. CURE: wire `loading` (dim + `aria-busy`) and give doSimplify doCompute's banner. **F.W1 benefit rider**: 7.0.0 `Metric` ships `loading` + `aria-busy`.
- **EV-C·D-02 (+D·D-M7)** ⟨EquationView⟩ MAJOR — **the server's only field-level diagnostic is destructured away before any consumer can reach it**: `api-problem.ts:37` names `detail` in the destructure (removing it from `...extensions`), then `typeof detail === "string"` DROPS FastAPI's array-shaped `{"detail":[{loc,msg,type}]}`; `title` falls back to `response.statusText` (`""` over HTTP/2 ⇒ `error = ""` ⇒ both banners' truthiness gates fail — SILENCE); `:123` reads `.message` only; `main.py` has ONE catch-all handler and no problem+json on the equations router's own 422/504/500. Narrowing adopted: the middleware's rate-limit 429 IS problem+json, so that one arm carries a non-empty title. **⊘ GATING RIDER: the error banner (raw `text-red-400`, no `title`/expand/wrap, no retry affordance) is REACHABLE ONLY when C·D-02 yields a non-empty string — the two defects cannot both bite in one deployment and either cure changes the other's reachability. ONE edit.** Palette-literal limb folds → FR-AUL-7.
- **EV-L·M-3/C·C-29** ⟨EquationView⟩ MAJOR — **two independent sessionStorage records, two write cadences, no shared key**: the input record rewrites on EVERY keystroke (un-debounced); the result record writes only on success; `CachedResult` carries NO compute key. Restore seeds `lastComputeKey`/`lastDisplayKey` from the NEW inputs while `result` restores OLD; the guard tests NULLITY only ⇒ **no corrective recompute EVER fires**; the early-return suppresses; and `displayLatex` restores POST-simplify while `latex_sigma` restores PRE-simplify — **three provenances, one screen** (the quantitatively-wrong-plot half: old coefficients under a new ω).
- **EV-L·M-2 (+I-2 witness) ⊕ L·M-7** ⟨EquationView⟩ MAJOR — abort-race clobbers the loading flag: `abortable(key)` aborts the prior controller synchronously before the first await under FIXED keys (`eq-compute`/`eq-simplify`), so A's `finally` clears `computing` while B is still in flight — **a whole network round trip with the template fallen through to the EMPTY state mid-compute**, reachable from cold load. Scope sharpening: doSimplify's identical shape is consequence-FREE today because `simplifying` has no template consumer — **the two halves are NOT equal in blast radius.** And there is **NO teardown anywhere** (zero `onUnmounted`/`onScopeDispose` on a lazily-loaded route component) while `abortInflight(keys)` exists with exactly 4 call sites, all in `stores/workspace.ts` — **the equation route is the one async surface not using it**; a dead instance's resolved handler writes six refs and `saveCachedResult` AFTER a fresh instance's `loadCachedResult`. CURE: generation token or per-call guard in `finally` + `onScopeDispose(() => abortInflight(["eq-compute","eq-simplify"]))`. **⊘ I-2 PROTECTION: the empty state (`:321-325`) is nearly unreachable in the shipped default — L·M-2's abort race is the ONE path that reaches it, making that branch this defect's ONLY visible symptom. An F.W4 pass MUST NOT delete it as "unused".**

### I · the `/paper` route — fr-PaperView · fr-PaperSidebar · fr-PaperArticleWindow · fr-PaperSearchModal · fr-PaperSearchDropdown · fr-InfoCard · fr-Tooltip · fr-SvgFilters · fr-CollapsibleSection

- **PSM-1 (= PV D/B-1 = C-01b)** ⟨PaperSearchModal D-1/L-1/C-1; PaperView D/B-1⟩ **BLOCKER** — **every CSS rule governing the modal is orphaned by Vue scoping; it ships as an UNSTYLED block appended to `<body>`**: the subject has zero `<style>` blocks, all 43 dead rules live in `PaperSearch.vue:41 <style scoped>` (**48 blocks / 5 live / 43 dead**), the scope id attaches to PaperSearch alone, and the `setScopeId` root-inheritance gate cannot fire because the subject's subTree is the Teleport vnode. Provenance: a refactor casualty at `ffba307`. The sibling damage (Input's descendants, the fragment-rooted Dropdown inheriting nothing, the input chrome) is **ONE repair, not three** — PaperView's `~35 of 41` orphaned groups is the same fact from the parent seat. **⊘ THREE MANDATORY SAME-COMMIT RIDERS: (1) PSM-13 — `.search-modal-input { outline: none }` is inert TODAY ONLY BECAUSE PSM-1 kills it; the moment the repair colocates the block, the modal's primary control loses its focus indicator with NO replacement (no `:focus`/`:focus-visible` for it anywhere; the global fallback enumerates exactly four other classes; the result rows keep glass Button's `focus-ring`, so the one control the user types into would be the ONLY ringless one) — the FIRST row in the registry where the REPAIR arms the defect. (2) PSM-4 — the four non-chrome surfaces fail 1.4.3/1.4.11 by ~2.4× in BOTH themes (1.88:1 hint light / 2.39:1 dark / 2.04:1 empty / 1.74:1 placeholder, reproduced to the digit by both readers calibrating against the producer's own 5.21/7.64 comments): MUST land in the same wave or the cure ships a WCAG regression. (3) PSM-12 — dark-mode figure/ground collapse survives as a named failure: the 1.5 px `--border` hairline at 2.82:1, below the 3:1 floor.** Rendered appearance → SS-13 #1.
- **PSM-2 ⊕ PSM-6 ⊕ PSM-3 (= PV D/B-2 = C-01 = L/D4 = PaperSidebar L-2/C-B3)** ⟨PaperSearchModal⟩ **BLOCKER** — **it is not a dialog**: zero `role=`/`aria-` tokens across the whole search family, no accessible name, no focus trap, no focus restore, no `inert`/`aria-hidden` background, no scroll lock, no combobox/listbox/`aria-activedescendant` semantics for the virtual selection it implements, Escape input-bound only — **while the house idiom exists two files away** (`MobileFloatingToc.vue:37-59`: focus restore + scroll lock + unmount restore). `@keydown` is bound to exactly ONE node (the input) while every result row and both header actions are native tabbable `<button>`s, so **the moment focus lands on any of up to 32 of them, ↑/↓/Enter/Escape all die while the footer keeps advertising them.** And **below 1024 px TWO modal instances mount simultaneously against one shared `isExpanded`** (one `usePaperSearch()`, two unconditional `<PaperSearch>` hosts, each teleporting out of a hidden subtree): both `nextTick` autofocuses race and `document.querySelector(".search-modal-results")` deterministically drives the **sidebar's INVISIBLE instance** by body-append order. **⊘ CURE: ONE modal owner mounted at PaperView altitude; the two hosts keep only triggers** (the mobile-scrolled arm is unconditional; the desktop arm rides the R-2 premise). Identity guard: intake R3-11/X-6 owns the Codex registry cell; this books the component instance.
- **PSM-13 ⊕ PSM-4 ⊕ PSM-12** — see the PSM-1 rider lock above; **none of the three may be scheduled independently of the colocation commit.**
- **PSM-5 ⊕ PSM-30** ⟨PaperSearchModal L-8/C-5⟩ MAJOR — **the prefix-narrowing cache stores the TRUNCATED top-30 then narrows the next keystroke's candidates from it** ⇒ a row falling out of a prefix's top-30 is PERMANENTLY unreachable down that keystroke path, and **typing vs pasting the same query returns different result sets**; the exported `maxResults` parameter is additionally inert on the cache-hit path. **Decisive producer diff verified in bytes: `search.js:110` caches the FULL scored array and slices only the return; `:85` re-slices on cache hit — the fork diverged by ONE `.slice()` placement.**
- **PSM-14** ⟨PaperSearchModal, reader-2 missed, seat-CONFIRMED BY EXECUTION⟩ MAJOR — **full-text search is functionally DEAD for short queries, and the prescribed producer adoption does NOT fix it**: `fuzzyMatch`'s length penalty scales with the FIELD while bonuses scale with the PATTERN, and `scoreEntry` drops any entry with `best <= 0`. Executed over a 458-char body: `"fo"` → **−31.6**, `"conv"` → **−36.4**, `"fourier"` (an exact word-boundary occurrence mid-body) → **−1.1**, `"convergence"` → +6.3. **⊘ THE RELAY LETTER MATTERS AS MUCH AS THE MIGRATION — the shared scoring formula carries the defect into the producer.**
- **PSM-9** ⟨PaperSearchModal D-9/C-4⟩ MAJOR (census escalation 🟡→🔴 HARD) — `@mkbabb/glass-ui/search` at the **INSTALLED 4.0.0** is a generalisation of this exact fork (same seven-line scoring docstring, same separator set, same 120/30 defaults, same ten state members — **different ORDER, so C-4's "same order" is FALSE** — prop-for-prop host component, byte-identical geometry/strings/`<kbd>` legend/row-key shape) **plus what the fork lacks: `useTextHighlight`, `clearSearchCache` + `onScopeDispose`, WeakMap-per-index.** The adoption seat is live at the pin.
- **PSM-7 (+PSM-39)** ⟨PaperSearchModal L-6⟩ MAJOR — **`SearchEntry.id` is not unique**: code and proof entries take `id: sectionId` unconditionally, so `selectResult → navigateTo(r.id)` sends the SECOND proof/code block in a section to the section heading, indistinguishable from the first. **⊘ The `:key` sub-claim DEMOTES: index-spliced keys are positional but the rows are stateless and NO stable identity EXISTS to key on — the cure MINTS one at `buildSearchIndex`, then deletes the index term.**
- **PSM-8 ⊕ PSM-10 ⊕ PSM-11 ⊕ PSM-27 ⊕ PSM-28 ⊕ PSM-31** ⟨PaperSearchModal⟩ MAJOR/MINOR — **no idle state** (an empty query paints "No results"; no busy affordance across the 120 ms debounce; no error branch) · `@mouseenter` writes `selectedIndex` with **no movement guard** while the list re-renders per keystroke ⇒ pointer travel HIJACKS keyboard selection (source-certain; the stationary/`scrollIntoView` half is engine re-hit-testing → SS-13) · `SearchResult.matches` is computed, spread onto every result and **read by nobody**, while the highlighter re-runs the full fuzzy match + per-character three-regex escaping inline in the `v-for` body on every render and per `mouseenter` — **root cause: `:185` indexes the LOWER-CASED label while `resultLabel` returns original case or a slice, so the indices genuinely misalign; the re-run is a correct workaround for a broken contract never deleted** · `close()` is not synchronous (query cleared but `debouncedQuery`/`results` lag 120 ms and `_cache` clears only on the next empty call) ⇒ **reopening within the window paints the PREVIOUS search's rows under an empty input** · **`highlightFuzzy` applies UTF-16 code-unit indices to a CODE-POINT array — executed: `"𝔽ourier basis"/"basis"` off-by-one; `"İstanbul set"/"set"` catastrophic**; third-order, `resultLabel`'s code-unit `.slice(0,120)` can emit a lone surrogate that `escapeHtml` passes to the sink · the index is built eagerly and synchronously in the composable's setup body on **every PaperView mount, search opened or not** (five `toLowerCase()` per entry) while the producer defers via `computed` — **on a route that carries a dedicated performance spec.**
- **PSM-15 ⊕ -16 ⊕ -17 ⊕ -18 ⊕ -19 ⊕ -20 ⊕ -21 ⊕ -22 ⊕ -23 ⊕ -25 ⊕ -26 ⊕ -29 ⊕ -32 ⊕ -34 ⊕ -35 ⊕ -37 ⊕ -38 ⊕ -40 ⊕ -41 ⊕ -42** ⟨PaperSearchModal⟩ MINOR sweep — `document.querySelector` where the sibling uses a template ref (**becomes WRONG under PSM-3**) · a bound `select` emit never invoked with no listener · `TYPE_LABELS: Record<string,string>` defeating exhaustiveness over a closed 13-member union with a declared-never-produced member · 20 lines of result-row markup duplicated verbatim and already drifted · three metric defects in one box (0.125 rem axis misalignment; off-`--radius` panel geometry; keycaps at 9.6 px inside PSM-4's 1.88:1) · three hardcoded HSL literals with no dark arm one rule block from the correct `color-mix(var(--primary))` idiom · `vh` vs `dvw` incoherence · **module-global query-keyed cache never index-keyed and never disposed + a debounce timer never cleared on scope dispose** (producer contrast: WeakMap + `clearSearchCache` + `onScopeDispose`) · `title=` where the house uses `aria-label=` (census **60 `aria-label` / 46 `title=`**) on a surface whose primary path is touch · **glass-ui ships a `.kbd` global utility and the producer's own FuzzySearch puts `class:"kbd"` on these EXACT four legend keycaps — fourier's four `<kbd>`s carry no class and hand-roll strictly worse values on every axis** · **two visually identical adjacent ghost buttons with OPPOSITE data semantics** (Collapse preserves the query; Close DESTROYS it, no undo) distinguished only by a `title` at an icon size that renders identically — **and the legend LIES: Escape COLLAPSES and never closes, so "esc close" is false as authored** · `sectionId`/`depth` are write-only payload shipped on every spread-copied result and retained in the cache (and `sectionId` means two different things at two site classes) · under PRM the enter-from frame still applies statically then snaps · no `type="button"` on any Button · equation rows display raw TeX hard-cut at 120 code units mid-token · contract asymmetries (no `variant` prop, so **the modal cannot self-arbitrate PSM-3's duplicate**;
